- **ref-long-documents-50pp**: Use as a reference guide for processing legal documents that exceed approximately 50 pages — the point at which direct full-text prompting becomes inefficient or exceeds context window limits. Applies to due diligence packages, M&A data rooms, long-form contracts, regulatory filings, and litigation bundles.
